# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-28)

## Corpus Check
- 7203 files · ~17,282,934 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 536 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 402, (none) 41, .css 16)

## Summary
- 71868 nodes · 211173 edges · 1551 communities (1411 shown, 140 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8790 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `26a5d934`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
